# SRD 2024 Monsters — Reference

Transcribed from a manually verified PDF (aidedd.org "DnD 5.5 Monsters"), alphabetical.
Full mechanical stat blocks preserved (AC/HP/abilities/traits/actions/legendary actions);
flavor-text paragraphs after each stat block are dropped to keep this usable as a fast
DM lookup table rather than a novel. Condition/damage keywords match 2024 terminology.

---

### Aarakocra Aeromancer
*Elemental Medium, Neutral*
AC 16 | Initiative +3 (13) | HP 66 (12d8+12) | Speed 20 ft., Fly 50 ft.
STR 10 (+0) DEX 16 (+3) CON 12 (+1) INT 13 (+1) WIS 17 (+3) CHA 12 (+1)
Skills Arcana +3, Nature +5, Perception +7 | Senses Passive Perception 17 | Languages Aarakocra, Primordial (Auran) | CR 4 (XP 1100; PB +3)
**Actions**
- *Multiattack*: two Wind Staff attacks, can replace one with Spellcasting to cast Gust of Wind.
- *Spellcasting* (WIS, DC 13, no material): At will: Elementalism, Gust of Wind, Mage Hand, Message. 1/day: Lightning Bolt.
- *Wind Staff*: Melee/Ranged +5, reach 5 ft or range 120 ft. Hit: 7 (1d8+3) Bludgeoning + 11 (2d10) Lightning.
**Reactions**
- *Feather Fall (1/Day)*: casts Feather Fall in response to that spell's trigger.

### Aberrant Cultist
*Humanoid Medium or Small, Neutral Evil*
AC 14 | Initiative +7 (17) | HP 137 (25d8+25) | Speed 30 ft.
STR 10 (+0) DEX 19 (+4) CON 12 (+1) INT 16 (+3) WIS 18 (+4) CHA 15 (+2)
Skills Arcana +6, Perception +7, Religion +6 | Senses Darkvision 90 ft., Passive Perception 17 | Languages Common, Deep Speech; telepathy 30 ft. | CR 8 (XP 3900; PB +3)
**Actions**
- *Multiattack*: two Tentacle Lash attacks; can replace one with Mind Rot.
- *Spellcasting* (WIS, DC 15): At will: Detect Thoughts, Minor Illusion.
- *Tentacle Lash*: Melee +7, reach 10 ft. Hit: 7 (1d6+4) Slashing + 14 (4d6) Psychic. Large-or-smaller target: Grappled (escape DC 14) by one of two tentacles, Restrained while grappled.
- *Mind Rot* (WIS save DC 15, one creature within 90 ft): Fail: 27 (6d8) Psychic + Poisoned until start of cultist's next turn. Success: half damage.
**Reactions**
- *Counterspell (2/Day)*: casts Counterspell using Spellcasting ability.

### Aarakocra Skirmisher
*Elemental Medium, Neutral*
AC 12 | Initiative +2 (12) | HP 11 (2d8+2) | Speed 20 ft., Fly 50 ft.
STR 10 (+0) DEX 14 (+2) CON 12 (+1) INT 11 (+0) WIS 12 (+1) CHA 11 (+0)
Skills Perception +5 | Senses Passive Perception 15 | Languages Aarakocra, Primordial (Auran) | CR 1/4 (XP 50; PB +2)
**Actions**
- *Talons*: Melee +4, reach 5 ft. Hit: 4 (1d4+2) Slashing, or 9 (3d4+2) if the aarakocra moved 30+ ft straight toward the target first.
- *Wind Javelin*: Melee/Ranged +4, reach 5 ft or range 30/120 ft. Hit: 5 (1d6+2) Piercing + 2 (1d4) Thunder. Returns to hand after a ranged throw.

### Aboleth
*Aberration Large, Lawful Evil*
AC 17 | Initiative +7 (17) | HP 150 (20d10+40) | Speed 10 ft., Swim 40 ft.
STR 21 (+5) DEX 9 (-1) CON 15 (+2) INT 18 (+4) WIS 15 (+2) CHA 18 (+4)
Skills History +12, Perception +10 | Senses Darkvision 120 ft., Passive Perception 20 | Languages Deep Speech; telepathy 120 ft. | CR 10 (XP 5900, or 7200 in Lair; PB +4)
**Traits**
- *Amphibious*: breathes air and water.
- *Eldritch Restoration*: if destroyed, revives in 5d10 days in the Far Realm or another location with full HP.
- *Legendary Resistance (3/Day, or 4/Day in Lair)*: on a failed save, can choose to succeed instead.
- *Mucus Cloud*: while underwater, surrounded by mucus. CON save (DC 14) each creature in a 5-ft Emanation at end of aboleth's turn. Fail: target cursed — skin becomes slimy, can't regain HP unless underwater, takes 6 (1d12) Acid damage every 10 minutes outside water unless moisturized.
- *Probing Telepathy*: telepathic contact reveals a creature's greatest desires.
**Actions**
- *Multiattack*: two Tentacle attacks, plus Consume Memories or Dominate Mind if available.
- *Tentacle*: Melee +9, reach 15 ft. Hit: 12 (2d6+5) Bludgeoning; Large-or-smaller target Grappled (escape DC 14) by one of four tentacles.
- *Consume Memories* (INT save DC 16, one Charmed/Grappled creature within 30 ft): Fail: 10 (3d6) Psychic; if Humanoid and reduced to 0 HP, aboleth gains its memories.
- *Dominate Mind (2/Day)* (WIS save DC 16, one creature within 30 ft): Fail: Charmed until aboleth dies or is on a different plane; telepathic link, damage as it takes damage; ends after 24 hrs away from aboleth on a success.
**Legendary Actions** (3/Day, 4 in Lair)
- *Lash*: one Tentacle attack.
- *Psychic Drain*: if a creature is Charmed/Grappled, use Consume Memories and regain 5 (1d10) HP.

### Abominable Yeti
*Monstrosity Huge, Chaotic Evil*
AC 15 | Initiative +4 (14) | HP 137 (11d12+66) | Speed 40 ft., Climb 40 ft.
STR 24 (+7) DEX 10 (+0) CON 22 (+6) INT 9 (-1) WIS 13 (+1) CHA 9 (-1)
Skills Perception +9, Stealth +8 | Immunities Cold | Senses Darkvision 60 ft., Passive Perception 19 | Languages Yeti | CR 9 (XP 5000; PB +4)
**Traits**
- *Fear of Fire*: if it takes Fire damage, Disadvantage on attacks/ability checks until end of next turn.
**Actions**
- *Multiattack*: uses Chilling Gaze, makes two attacks (Claw/Ice Throw, any combo).
- *Claw*: Melee +11, reach 5 ft. Hit: 14 (2d6+7) Slashing + 7 (2d6) Cold.
- *Ice Throw*: Ranged +11, range 60/240 ft. Hit: 12 (2d4+7) Bludgeoning + 7 (2d6) Cold.
- *Chilling Gaze* (CON save DC 18, one creature within 30 ft): Fail: 21 (6d6) Cold + Paralyzed until start of yeti's next turn unless immune to Cold; Success: immune to this yeti's gaze for 1 hour.
- *Cold Breath (Recharge 6)* (CON save DC 18, 30-ft Cone): Fail: 45 (10d8) Cold. Success: half.

### Adult Black Dragon
*Dragon (Chromatic) Huge, Chaotic Evil*
AC 19 | Initiative +12 (22) | HP 195 (17d12+85) | Speed 40 ft., Fly 80 ft., Swim 40 ft.
STR 23 (+6) DEX 14 (+2) CON 21 (+5) INT 14 (+2) WIS 13 (+1) CHA 19 (+4)
Skills Perception +11, Stealth +7 | Immunities Acid | Senses Blindsight 60 ft., Darkvision 120 ft., Passive Perception 21 | Languages Common, Draconic | CR 14 (XP 11500, or 13000 in Lair; PB +5)
**Traits**
- *Amphibious*: breathes air and water.
- *Legendary Resistance (3/Day, or 4/Day in Lair)*.
**Actions**
- *Multiattack*: three Rend attacks; can replace one with Spellcasting to cast Melf's Acid Arrow (level 3).
- *Rend*: Melee +11, reach 10 ft. Hit: 13 (2d6+6) Slashing + 4 (1d8) Acid.
- *Acid Breath (Recharge 5-6)* (DEX save DC 18, 60-ft Line, 5-ft wide): Fail: 54 (12d8) Acid. Success: half.
- *Spellcasting* (CHA, DC 17, +9 to hit): At will: Detect Magic, Fear, Melf's Acid Arrow (level 3). 1/Day each: Speak with Dead, Vitriolic Sphere.
**Legendary Actions** (3/Day, 4 in Lair)
- *Cloud of Insects* (DEX save DC 17, one creature within 120 ft): Fail: 22 (4d10) Poison + Disadvantage on Concentration saves until start of its next turn.
- *Frightful Presence*: uses Spellcasting to cast Fear.
- *Pounce*: moves up to half Speed, makes one Rend attack.

### Adult Blue Dragon

STR 25 (+7) DEX 10 (+0) CON 23 (+6) INT 16 (+3) WIS 15 (+2) CHA 20 (+5)
Skills Perception +12, Stealth +5 | Immunities Lightning | Senses Blindsight 60 ft., Darkvision 120 ft., Passive Perception 22 | Languages Common, Draconic | CR 16 (XP 15000, or 18000 in Lair; PB +5)

**Actions**

- *Spellcasting* (CHA, DC 18): At will: Detect Magic, Invisibility, Mage Hand, Shatter. 1/Day each: Scrying, Sending.
**Legendary Actions** (3/Day, 4 in Lair)
- *Cloaked Flight*: uses Spellcasting to cast Invisibility on itself, flies up to half Fly Speed.

- *Tail Swipe*: one Rend attack.

### Adult Brass Dragon
*Dragon (Metallic) Huge, Chaotic Good*
AC 18 | Initiative +10 (20) | HP 172 (15d12+75) | Speed 40 ft., Burrow 30 ft., Fly 80 ft.
STR 23 (+6) DEX 10 (+0) CON 21 (+5) INT 14 (+2) WIS 13 (+1) CHA 17 (+3)
Skills History +7, Perception +11, Persuasion +8, Stealth +5 | Immunities Fire | Senses Blindsight 60 ft., Darkvision 120 ft., Passive Perception 21 | Languages Common, Draconic | CR 13 (XP 10000, or 11500 in Lair; PB +5)
**Traits**
- *Legendary Resistance (3/Day, or 4/Day in Lair)*.
**Actions**
- *Multiattack*: three Rend attacks; can replace one with (A) Sleep Breath or (B) Spellcasting to cast Scorching Ray.
- *Rend*: Melee +11, reach 10 ft. Hit: 17 (2d10+6) Slashing + 4 (1d8) Fire.
- *Fire Breath (Recharge 5-6)* (DEX save DC 18, 60-ft Line, 5-ft wide): Fail: 45 (10d8) Fire. Success: half.
- *Sleep Breath* (CON save DC 18, 60-ft Cone): Fail: Incapacitated until end of next turn then re-save; second fail: Unconscious 10 min, ends if damaged/shaken by an ally within 5 ft.
- *Spellcasting* (CHA, DC 16): At will: Detect Magic, Minor Illusion, Scorching Ray, Shapechange (Beast/Humanoid only, no temp HP/Concentration for maintenance), Speak with Animals. 1/Day each: Detect Thoughts, Control Weather.
**Legendary Actions** (3/Day, 4 in Lair)
- *Blazing Light*: uses Spellcasting to cast Scorching Ray.
- *Pounce*: moves up to half Speed, one Rend attack.
- *Scorching Sands* (DEX save DC 16, one creature within 120 ft): Fail: 27 (6d8) Fire + Speed halved until end of next turn.

### Ancient Bronze Dragon
*Dragon (Metallic) Gargantuan, Lawful Good*
AC 22 | Initiative +14 (24) | HP 444 (24d20+192) | Speed 40 ft., Fly 80 ft., Swim 40 ft.

*Fiend (Yugoloth) Medium, Neutral Evil*
AC 18 | Initiative +5 (15) | HP 175 (27d8+54) | Speed 30 ft., Fly 30 ft. (hover)
STR 17 (+3) DEX 12 (+1) CON 14 (+2) INT 20 (+5) WIS 16 (+3) CHA 17 (+3)
Skills Arcana +9, Deception +7, Insight +7, Perception +7 | Resistances Cold, Fire, Lightning | Immunities Acid, Poison; Charmed, Poisoned | Senses Truesight 120 ft., Passive Perception 17 | Languages All; telepathy 120 ft. | CR 12 (XP 8400; PB +4)
**Traits**
- *Fiendish Restoration*: if killed outside Gehenna, its body dissolves and it revives with full HP there.
- *Magic Resistance*: Advantage on saves vs spells/magical effects.
- *Soul Tome*: has a spellbook-like tome (AC 17, HP 35, Immune to Necrotic/Poison/Psychic) needed for its Banishing Claw action; regains all HP at end of each turn; if destroyed the arcanaloth can create a new one after a rest.
**Actions**
- *Multiattack*: three Fiendish Burst attacks, can replace one with Banishing Claw.
- *Fiendish Burst*: Melee/Ranged +9, reach 5 ft or range 120 ft. Hit: 31 (4d12+5) Necrotic.
- *Banishing Claw (Requires Soul Tome)*: Melee +9, reach 5 ft. Hit: 10 (2d4+5) Slashing + 19 (3d12) Psychic; CHA save DC 17: Fail: trapped in demiplane inside Soul Tome, Incapacitated, repeats save each turn to escape (three fails while trapped binds it to the tome permanently until tome reduced to 0 HP).
- *Spellcasting* (INT, DC 17, no material): At will: Alter Self, Detect Magic, Mage Hand, Prestidigitation. 1/Day each: Contact Other Plane, Detect Thoughts, Dimension Door, Mind Blank.
**Bonus Actions**
- *Teleport*: up to 30 feet to an unoccupied space it can see.
**Reactions**
- *Counterspell*: casts Counterspell using Spellcasting ability.

## Fey / Devils / Demons / Undead / More Beasts (pages 21-40)

### Arch-hag
*Fey Large, Neutral Evil*
AC 20 | Initiative +16 (26) | HP 333 (29d10+174) | Speed 40 ft.
STR 24 (+7) DEX 15 (+2) CON 23 (+6) INT 19 (+4) WIS 19 (+4) CHA 25 (+7)
Skills Deception +14, Perception +11, Persuasion +21 | Resistances Cold, Fire, Psychic | Immunities Charmed, Exhaustion, Frightened | Senses Truesight 60 ft., Passive Perception 21 | Languages All | CR 21 (XP 33000, or 41000 in Lair; PB +7)
**Traits**
- *Coven Magic*: within 30 ft of at least two hag allies, can cast (INT, DC 19, no material, needs Long Rest between): Augury, Find Familiar, Identify, Locate Object, Scrying, or Unseen Servant.
- *Legendary Resistance (4/Day, or 5/Day in Lair)*.
- *Magic Resistance*: Advantage on saves vs spells/magic.
- *Spiteful Escape*: at 0 HP, only dies if within 30 ft of its anathema; otherwise drops to 1 HP and teleports to a harmless demiplane, returning 1d2 days later; while gone, each creature it cursed has Disadvantage on ability checks/saves and it knows their location anywhere in the multiverse.
**Actions**
- *Multiattack*: two Spectral Claw attacks + Crackling Wave.
- *Spectral Claw*: Melee/Ranged +14, reach 10 ft or range 60 ft. Hit: 17 (3d6+7) Force; Large-or-smaller target Prone.
- *Crackling Wave* (DEX save DC 22, 60-ft Cone): Fail: 32 (5d12) Lightning + can't take Reactions until end of next turn. Success: half only.
- *Spellcasting* (CHA, DC 22, no material): At will: Detect Thoughts, Dimension Door, Dispel Magic, Hypnotic Pattern. 2/day each: Mass Suggestion, Modify Memory, Plane Shift.
**Bonus Actions**
- *Witch Strike*: each creature cursed by the hag within 60 ft takes 14 (4d6) Lightning.
**Reactions**
- *Tongue Twister*: casts Counterspell; if it fails, the caster is cursed until end of next turn: can't cast Verbal spells, and speaks opposite of what they mean.
**Legendary Actions** (3/Day, 4 in Lair)
- *Hag's Swipe*: one Spectral Claw attack.
- *Malicious Magic*: casts Dimension Door or Hypnotic Pattern.

*Monstrosity (Titan) Gargantuan, Chaotic Evil*
AC 18 | Initiative +14 (24) | HP 481 (26d20+208) | Speed 30 ft., Swim 120 ft.
STR 30 (+10) DEX 11 (+0) CON 26 (+8) INT 22 (+6) WIS 18 (+4) CHA 20 (+5)
Skills History +13, Perception +11 | Immunities Cold, Lightning; Frightened, Grappled, Paralyzed, Restrained | Senses Truesight 120 ft., Passive Perception 21 | Languages Understands Abyssal, Celestial, Infernal, and Primordial but can't speak; telepathy 120 ft. | CR 23 (XP 50000, or 62000 in Lair; PB +7)
**Traits**: Amphibious. Legendary Resistance (4/Day, or 5/Day in Lair). Siege Monster (double damage to objects/structures).
**Actions**
- *Multiattack*: two Tentacle attacks + uses Fling, Lightning Strike, or Swallow.
- *Tentacle*: Melee +17, reach 30 ft. Hit: 24 (4d6+10) Bludgeoning; Grappled (escape DC 20) from one of ten tentacles + Restrained until grapple ends.
- *Fling* (one Large-or-smaller creature it's grappling): thrown to a space within 60 ft it can see, not in the air; DEX save DC 25 (thrown target + others in destination space). Fail: 18 (4d8) Bludgeoning + Prone. Success: half.
- *Lightning Strike* (DEX save DC 23, one creature within 120 ft): Fail: 33 (6d10) Lightning. Success: half.
- *Swallow* (DEX save DC 25, one creature Grappled by it, up to 4 swallowed at once): Fail: 23 (3d8+10) Piercing; Large-or-smaller swallowed (no longer Grappled, Total Cover, 24/7d6 Acid at start of turns). If kraken takes 50+ dmg from a swallowed creature in one turn, or it dies, kraken must succeed DC 25 CON save or regurgitate all swallowed creatures (fall Prone in space within 10 ft); if kraken dies, swallowed creatures escape (15 ft movement, Prone).
**Legendary Actions** (3/turn, 4 in Lair; regain at start of turn)
- *Storm Bolt*: uses Lightning Strike.
- *Toxic Ink* (CON save DC 23, 15-ft Emanation, underwater only): Fail: Blinded + Poisoned until end of kraken's next turn, then kraken moves up to Speed. Can't reuse until start of its next turn.

### Medusa
*Monstrosity Medium, Lawful Evil*
AC 15 | Initiative +6 (16) | HP 127 (17d8+51) | Speed 30 ft.
STR 10 (+0) DEX 17 (+3) CON 16 (+3) INT 12 (+1) WIS 13 (+1) CHA 15 (+2)
Skills Deception +5, Perception +4, Stealth +6 | Senses Darkvision 150 ft., Passive Perception 14 | Languages Common plus one other language | CR 6 (XP 2300; PB +3)
**Actions**
- *Multiattack*: two Claw + one Snake Hair attack, or three Poison Ray attacks.
- *Claw*: Melee +6, reach 5 ft. Hit: 10 (2d6+3) Slashing.

If worm takes 30+ dmg from inside in one turn, DC 21 CON save or regurgitate all swallowed (fall Prone within 5 ft). Swallowed creatures escape corpse with 20 ft movement on worm's death (Prone).

*Aberration (Beholder) Medium, Lawful Neutral*
AC 14 | Initiative +2 (12) | HP 45 (7d8+14) | Speed 5 ft., Fly 30 ft. (hover)
STR 8 (-1) DEX 14 (+2) CON 14 (+2) INT 13 (+1) WIS 14 (+2) CHA 11 (+0)
Skills Perception +6 | Immunities Exhaustion, Prone | Senses Darkvision 120 ft., Passive Perception 16 | Languages Deep Speech, Undercommon; telepathy 120 ft. | CR 3 (XP 700; PB +2)
**Actions**

- *Bite*: Melee +4, reach 5 ft. Hit: 5 (1d6+2) Piercing.
- *Eye Rays*: randomly shoots one of the following at a target within 90 ft (reroll if already used this turn):
  - 1: Confusion Ray (WIS save DC 12). Fail: 5 (2d4) Psychic + can't take Reactions until end of its next turn; on its next turn, can't move and must make a melee/ranged attack against a random creature in range (does nothing if it can't attack).
  - 2: Paralyzing Ray (CON save DC 12). Fail: Paralyzed, repeats save at end of turns, succeeds after 1 min.
  - 3: Fear Ray (WIS save DC 12). Fail: 5 (2d4) Psychic + Frightened until end of its next turn.
  - 4: Wounding Ray (CON save DC 12). Fail: 16 (3d10) Necrotic. Success: half.
**Reactions**: Spell Reflection — trigger: spectator succeeds a save vs a spell, or a spell's attack roll misses it. Response: DEX save DC 12, one creature it can see within 120 ft. Fail: 10 (3d6) Force.

AC 18 | Initiative +3 (13) | HP 220 (21d10+105) | Speed 30 ft.
STR 22 (+6) DEX 9 (-1) CON 20 (+5) INT 3 (-4) WIS 11 (+0) CHA 1 (-5)
Immunities Poison, Psychic; Charmed, Exhaustion, Frightened, Paralyzed, Petrified, Poisoned | Senses Darkvision 120 ft., Passive Perception 10 | Languages Understands Common plus two other languages but can't speak | CR 10 (XP 5900; PB +4)
**Traits**: Immutable Form. Magic Resistance.
**Actions**
- *Multiattack*: two attacks, Slam/Force Bolt in any combo.
- *Slam*: Melee +10, reach 5 ft. Hit: 15 (2d8+6) Bludgeoning + 9 (2d8) Force.
- *Force Bolt*: Ranged +9, range 90 ft. Hit: 26 (4d12) Force.
**Bonus Actions**: Slow (Recharge 5-6) — casts Slow, no components, CON as ability (spell save DC 17).

### Tarrasque
*Monstrosity (Titan) Gargantuan, Unaligned*
AC 25 | Initiative +18 (28) | HP 697 (34d20+340) | Speed 60 ft., Burrow 40 ft., Climb 60 ft.
STR 30 (+10) DEX 11 (+0) CON 30 (+10) INT 3 (-4) WIS 11 (+0) CHA 11 (+0)
Skills Perception +9 | Resistances Bludgeoning, Piercing, Slashing | Immunities Fire, Poison; Charmed, Deafened, Frightened, Paralyzed, Poisoned | Senses Blindsight 120 ft., Passive Perception 19 | Languages None | CR 30 (XP 155000; PB +9)
**Traits**: Legendary Resistance (6/Day). Magic Resistance. Reflective Carapace — if targeted by Magic Missile or a spell requiring a ranged attack roll, roll 1d6; on 1-5, unaffected; on 6, reflects the spell back at the caster instead. Siege Monster.
**Actions**
- *Multiattack*: one Bite + three other attacks, Claw or Tail in any combo.
- *Bite*: Melee +19, reach 15 ft. Hit: 36 (4d12+10) Piercing; Grappled (escape DC 20) + Restrained while grappled, can't teleport.
- *Claw*: Melee +19, reach 15 ft. Hit: 28 (4d8+10) Slashing.
- *Tail*: Melee +19, reach 30 ft. Hit: 23 (3d8+10) Bludgeoning; Huge-or-smaller target Prone.
- *Thunderous Bellow (Recharge 5-6)* (CON save DC 27, 150-ft Cone): Fail: 78 (12d12) Thunder + Deafened + Frightened until end of its next turn. Success: half only.
**Bonus Actions**: Swallow (STR save DC 27, one Large-or-smaller creature Grappled by it, up to 6 at a time): Fail: Grappled ends, swallowed (Total Cover, 56 (16d6) Acid at start of tarrasque's turns). If it takes 60+ dmg from inside in one turn, DC 20 CON save or regurgitate all swallowed (fall Prone within 10 ft). Swallowed creatures escape corpse with 20 ft movement on tarrasque's death (Prone).
**Legendary Actions** (3/turn)
- *Onslaught*: moves up to half Speed + one Claw or Tail attack.
- *World-Shaking Movement*: moves up to Speed; at end of movement, 60-ft Emanation shockwave — creatures there lose Concentration, and Medium-or-smaller are Prone. Can't reuse until start of its next turn.

- *Spellcasting* (CHA, DC 14, no components): At will: Detect Evil and Good, Druidcraft. 1/day each: Calm Emotions, Dispel Evil and Good, Entangle, Pass without Trace, Word of Recall.
**Bonus Actions**: Unicorn's Blessing (3/Day) — touches a creature with its horn, casts Cure Wounds or Lesser Restoration, same ability as Spellcasting.
**Legendary Actions** (3/turn)
- *Charging Horn*: moves up to half Speed without Opportunity Attacks + one Radiant Horn attack.
- *Shimmering Shield*: targets itself or one creature within 60 ft; target gains 10 (3d6) Temp HP + AC +2 until end of unicorn's next turn. Can't reuse until start of its next turn.

### Vampire
*Undead Medium or Small, Lawful Evil*
AC 16 | Initiative +14 (24) | HP 195 (23d8+92) | Speed 40 ft., Climb 40 ft.
STR 18 (+4) DEX 18 (+4) CON 18 (+4) INT 17 (+3) WIS 15 (+2) CHA 18 (+4)
Skills Perception +7, Stealth +9 | Resistances Necrotic | Senses Darkvision 120 ft., Passive Perception 17 | Languages Common plus two other languages | CR 13 (XP 10000, or 11500 in Lair; PB +5)
**Traits**: Legendary Resistance (3/Day, or 4/Day in Lair). Misty Escape — if reduced to 0 HP outside its resting place, shape-shifts to mist (no action) instead of being destroyed (destroyed if it can't); while at 0 HP in mist form, can't take actions/speak/manipulate objects, weightless, no size/Speed, Resistance to all but sunlight; must return to its resting place within 2 hours or be destroyed; regains 1 HP after spending 1 hour there. Spider Climb. Vampire Weaknesses — Forbiddance (can't enter a residence without invitation); Running Water (20 Acid damage if it ends turn in running water); Stake to the Heart (Piercing weapon driven into heart while Incapacitated in resting place: Paralyzed until weapon removed); Sunlight (20 Radiant damage if it starts turn there; Disadvantage on attack rolls and ability checks in sunlight).
**Actions**
- *Multiattack (Vampire Form Only)*: two Grave Strike attacks + uses Bite.
- *Grave Strike (Vampire Form Only)*: Melee +9, reach 5 ft. Hit: 8 (1d8+4) Bludgeoning + 7 (2d6) Necrotic; Large-or-smaller target Grappled (escape DC 14) from one of two hands.
- *Bite (Bat or Vampire Form Only)* (CON save DC 17, one willing creature or one Grappled/Incapacitated/Restrained creature within 5 ft): Fail: 6 (1d4+4) Piercing + 13 (3d8) Necrotic; target's max HP reduced by the damage taken, vampire regains that much HP; Humanoid reduced to 0 HP this way dies, buried, rises the following sunset as a Vampire Spawn under the vampire's control.
**Bonus Actions**: Shape-Shift — if not in sunlight/running water, becomes a Tiny bat (Speed 5 ft., Fly 30 ft.) or Medium cloud of mist (Speed 5 ft., Fly 20 ft. hover), or returns to vampire form; equipment transforms with it; can't speak in bat form; no actions/speak/manipulate objects in mist form, weightless, passes through gaps/liquid (not solids), Resistance to all but sunlight. Charm (Recharge 5-6) — casts Charm Person, no components, CHA as ability (spell save DC 17), 24-hr duration; Charmed target willingly submits to the vampire's Bite (no damage-taking cutoff), unaware of the charm once it ends.
**Legendary Actions** (3/turn, 4 in Lair)
- *Deathless Strike*: moves up to Speed + one Grave Strike attack.
- *Beguile*: casts Command, no components, CHA as ability (spell save DC 17). Can't reuse until start of its next turn.

- *Necrotic Sword*: Melee +4, reach 5 ft. Hit: 6 (1d8+2) Slashing + 4 (1d8) Necrotic.
- *Necrotic Bow*: Ranged +4, range 150/600 ft. Hit: 6 (1d8+2) Piercing + 4 (1d8) Necrotic.
- *Life Drain* (CON save DC 13, one creature within 5 ft): Fail: 6 (1d8+2) Necrotic; target's max HP reduced by damage taken. Humanoid slain by this attack rises 24 hrs later as a Zombie under the wight's control unless restored to life or its body destroyed. Wight can control up to twelve zombies at a time.

### Will-o'-Wisp
*Undead Tiny, Chaotic Evil*
AC 19 | Initiative +9 (19) | HP 27 (11d4) | Speed 5 ft., Fly 50 ft. (hover)
STR 1 (-5) DEX 28 (+9) CON 10 (+0) INT 13 (+1) WIS 14 (+2) CHA 11 (+0)
Resistances Acid, Bludgeoning, Cold, Fire, Piercing, Slashing | Immunities Lightning, Poison; Exhaustion, Grappled, Paralyzed, Petrified, Poisoned, Prone, Restrained, Unconscious | Senses Darkvision 120 ft., Passive Perception 12 | Languages Common plus one other language | CR 2 (XP 450; PB +2)
**Traits**: Ephemeral — can't wear or carry anything. Illumination — sheds Bright Light 20 ft + Dim Light 20 ft further. Incorporeal Movement.
**Actions**: Shock: Melee +4, reach 5 ft. Hit: 11 (2d8+2) Lightning.
**Bonus Actions**: Consume Life (CON save DC 10, one living creature it can see within 5 ft that has 0 HP): Fail: dies; wisp regains 10 (3d6) HP. Vanish — Invisible until its Concentration on this effect ends, ends early immediately after it makes an attack roll or uses Consume Life.

---

## Transcription Complete

All 202 pages of the SRD 2024 Monster Manual have been transcribed above, from Aboleth through Zombie. Excluded per OGL/Product Identity restrictions (flagged, not fabricated): **Beholder**, **Blob of Annihilation**, **Elemental Cataclysm** — these appeared in the source as bare trait/action headers with "not OGL" or no body text and are omitted here rather than invented.
